\\\\\\\"libero_90\\\\\\\",\\\\n}\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef parse_args():\\\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\\\\\"Lerobot-native precompute for CRM training\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--policy_path\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero/\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--suite\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"object\\\\\\\",\\\\n                   choices=[\\\\\\\"object\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"spatial\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"goal\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"long\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"90\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--K\\\\\\\", type=int, default=10)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--n_episodes\\\\\\\", type=int, default=10)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--output_dir\\\\\\\", type=str, required=True)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--seed\\\\\\\", type=int, default=42)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--device\\\\\\\", type=str, default=\\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--max_steps\\\\\\\", type=int, default=None,\\\\n                   help=\\\\\\\"Override max steps per episode (default: suite-specific)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return p.parse_args()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef get_robot_state(obs):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"8D robot state: eef_pos(3) + eef_axisangle(3) + gripper_qpos(2).\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    eef_pos = obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    eef_axisangle = quat2axisangle(obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eef_quat\\\\\\\"])\\\\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_gripper_qpos\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_axisangle, gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float64)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_libero_env(suite_name):\\\\n    from libero.libero import benchmark as bm\\\\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\\\\n\\\\n    bm_name = BENCHMARK_MAP[suite_name]\\\\n    benchmark_instance = bm.get_benchmark(bm_name)()\\\\n\\\\n    task_envs = []\\\\n    for task_idx in range(benchmark_instance.n_tasks):\\\\n        task = benchmark_instance.get_task(task_idx)\\\\n        task_bddl = benchmark_instance.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_idx)\\\\n        env_args = {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"bddl_file_name\\\\\\\": task_bddl,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"camera_heights\\\\\\\": 256,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"camera_widths\\\\\\\": 256,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"camera_names\\\\\\\": [\\\\\\\"agentview\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            \\\\\\\"render_gpu_device_id\\\\\\\": int(os.environ.get(\\\\\\\"MUJOCO_EGL_DEVICE_ID\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"0\\\\\\\")),\\\\n        }\\\\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\\\\n        env.seed(42)\\\\n        task_envs.append({\\\\n            \\\\\\\"task_name\\\\\\\": task.name,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"task_description\\\\\\\": task.language,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"env\\\\\\\": env,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"init_states\\\\\\\": benchmark_instance.get_task_init_states(task_idx),\\\\n        })\\\\n    return task_envs\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass LerobotNativePrecomputer:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Precompute using lerobot's official SmolVLA inference path.\\\\n\\\\n    Replicates the exact preprocessing pipeline from lerobot eval:\\\\n      1. __init__(self, policy_path, device=\\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\"):\\\\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\\\\n\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"Loading SmolVLA from {policy_path}...\\\\\\\")\\\\n        self.policy = SmolVLAPolicy.from_pretrained(policy_path)\\\\n        self.policy.to(self.device)\\\\n        self.policy.eval()\\\\n        for p in self.policy.parameters():\\\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\\\n\\\\n        self.flow: VLAFlowMatching = self.policy.model\\\\n        self.config = self.policy.config\\\\n        self.processor = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.processor\\\\n        self.tokenizer = self.processor.tokenizer\\\\n\\\\n        model_dir = Path(policy_path)\\\\n        pre_stats = load_file(str(model_dir / \\\\\\\"policy_postprocessor_step_0_unnormalizer_processor.safetensors\\\\\\\"))\\\\n\\\\n        self.state_mean = pre_stats[\\\\\\\"observation.state.mean\\\\\\\"].to(self.device)\\\\n        self.state_std = pre_stats[\\\\\\\"observation.state.std\\\\\\\"].to(self.device)\\\\n        self.action_mean = post_stats[\\\\\\\"action.mean\\\\\\\"].to(self.device)\\\\n        self.action_std = post_stats[\\\\\\\"action.std\\\\\\\"].to(self.device)\\\\n\\\\n        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.config.text_config.hidden_size\\\\n        self._to_tensor = T.ToTensor()\\\\n\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"  hidden_size={self.vlm_hidden_size}, \\\\\\\"\\\\n              f\\\\\\\"expert_hidden_size={self.flow.vlm_with_expert.expert_hidden_size}, \\\\\\\"\\\\n              f\\\\\\\"chunk_size={self.config.chunk_size}, \\\\\\\"\\\\n              f\\\\\\\"action_dim={self.config.action_feature.shape[0]}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    def _normalize_state(self, state_raw):\\\\n        D = state_raw.shape[-1]\\\\n        return (state_raw - self.state_mean[:D]) / (self.state_std[:D] + 1e-8)\\\\n\\\\n    def _unnormalize_action(self, action_norm):\\\\n        return action_norm * self.action_std + self.action_mean\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def build_batch(self, obs, task_description):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Convert raw LIBERO obs -> lerobot batch dict.\\\\n\\\\n        Matches the lerobot preprocessor pipeline exactly:\\\\n          rename_observations -> to_batch -> smolvla_new_line -> tokenizer -> device -> normalizer\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        dev = self.device\\\\n        batch = {}\\\\n\\\\n        # Images: HWC uint8 -> CHW float [0,1] -- no flip (lerobot doesn't flip)\\\\n        agentview = self._to_tensor(obs[\\\\\\\"agentview_image\\\\\\\"]).unsqueeze(0).to(dev)\\\\n        batch[\\\\\\\"observation.images.camera1\\\\\\\"] = agentview\\\\n\\\\n        eye_in_hand = self._to_tensor(obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\"]).unsqueeze(0).to(dev)\\\\n        batch[\\\\\\\"observation.images.camera2\\\\\\\"] = eye_in_hand\\\\n\\\\n        # State: 8D -> MEAN_STD normalize\\\\n        robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\\\\n        state_t = torch.tensor(robot_state, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)\\\\n        state_norm = self._normalize_state(state_t)\\\\n        batch[OBS_STATE] = state_norm.unsqueeze(0)\\\\n\\\\n        # Language: append \\\\\\\\n then tokenize (matches smolvla_new_line_processor + tokenizer_processor)\\\\n        task_text = task_description if task_description.endswith(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\") else f\\\\\\\"{task_description}\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n        tokens = self.tokenizer(\\\\n            task_text,\\\\n            return_tensors=\\\\\\\"pt\\\\\\\",\\\\n            padding=\\\\\\\"max_length\\\\\\\",\\\\n            max_length=self.config.tokenizer_max_length,\\\\n            truncation=True,\\\\n        )\\\\n        batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS] = tokens[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"].to(dev)\\\\n        batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK] = tokens[\\\\\\\"attention_mask\\\\\\\"].to(dev).bool()\\\\n\\\\n        return batch\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def precompute_step(self, batch, K):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"One precompute step: obs -> (obs_features, K action chunks).\\\\n\\\\n        Uses lerobot's prepare_images + prepare_state, then:\\\\n          1. embed_prefix -> VLM forward -> obs_features (mean-pooled) + KV cache\\\\n          2. benchmark_instance = bm.get_benchmark(bm_name)()\\n\\n    task_envs = []\\n    for task_idx in range(benchmark_instance.n_tasks):\\n        task = benchmark_instance.get_task(task_idx)\\n \\\"camera_widths\\\": 256,\\n            \\\"camera_names\\\": [\\\"agentview\\\", \\\"robot0_eye_in_hand\\\"],\\n            \\\"render_gpu_device_id\\\": \\\"task_name\\\": task.name,\\n            \\\"task_description\\\": task.language,\\n            \\\"env\\\": env,\\n            \\\"init_states\\\": benchmark_instance.get_task_init_states(task_idx),\\n        })\\n    return task_envs\\n\\n\\nclass LerobotNativePrecomputer:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Precompute using lerobot's official SmolVLA inference post_stats = load_file(str(model_dir / \\\"policy_postprocessor_step_0_unnormalizer_processor.safetensors\\\"))\\n\\n        self.state_mean = post_stats[\\\"action.mean\\\"].to(self.device)\\n        self.action_std = post_stats[\\\"action.std\\\"].to(self.device)\\n\\n        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.config.text_config.hidden_size\\n        self._to_tensor = T.ToTensor()\\n\\n        print(f\\\"  hidden_size={self.vlm_hidden_size}, \\\"\\n              f\\\"expert_hidden_size={self.flow.vlm_with_expert.expert_hidden_size}, \\\"\\n              f\\\"chunk_size={self.config.chunk_size}, \\\"\\n              f\\\"action_dim={self.config.action_feature.shape[0]}\\\")\\n\\n    def _normalize_state(self, state_raw):\\n        D = state_raw.shape[-1]\\n        return (state_raw - self.state_mean[:D]) / (self.state_std[:D] + 1e-8)\\n\\n    def _unnormalize_action(self, action_norm):\\n        return action_norm * self.action_std + self.action_mean\\n\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def build_batch(self, obs, task_description):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert raw LIBERO obs -> lerobot batch dict.\\n\\n        Matches the lerobot preprocessor pipeline exactly:\\n          rename_observations -> to_batch -> smolvla_new_line -> tokenizer -> device -> normalizer\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        dev = self.device\\n        batch = {}\\n\\n        # Images: HWC uint8 -> CHW float [0,1] -- no flip (lerobot K x denoise loop with fresh noise -> K unnormalized action chunks\\\\n\\\\n        The KV cache stores the deterministic prefix representation. Only the\\\\n        action denoising noise varies across K samples, matching model.sample_actions\\\\n        semantics exactly.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        images, img_masks = self.policy.prepare_images(batch)\\\\n        state = self.policy.prepare_state(batch)\\\\n        lang_tokens = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS]\\\\n        lang_masks = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK]\\\\n\\\\n        # --- prefix encoding (run once) ---\\\\n        prefix_embs, prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks = self.flow.embed_prefix(\\\\n            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state=state\\\\n        )\\\\n        prefix_att_2d_masks = make_att_2d_masks(prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks)\\\\n        prefix_position_ids = torch.cumsum(prefix_pad_masks, dim=1) - 1\\\\n\\\\n        outputs_embeds, past_key_values = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.forward(\\\\n            attention_mask=prefix_att_2d_masks,\\\\n            position_ids=prefix_position_ids,\\\\n            past_key_values=None,\\\\n            inputs_embeds=[prefix_embs, None],\\\\n            use_cache=self.config.use_cache,\\\\n            fill_kv_cache=True,\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        # obs_features: mean-pool VLM hidden states over valid prefix tokens\\\\n        vlm_hidden = outputs_embeds[0]  # (B, seq_len, hidden_size)\\\\n        mask = prefix_pad_masks.unsqueeze(-1).float()\\\\n        obs_features = (vlm_hidden.float() * mask).sum(1) / mask.sum(1).clamp(min=1)\\\\n\\\\n        # --- K action samples via cached prefix ---\\\\n        B = prefix_pad_masks.shape[0]\\\\n        chunk_size = self.config.chunk_size\\\\n        max_action_dim = self.config.max_action_dim\\\\n        original_dim = self.config.action_feature.shape[0]\\\\n        num_steps = self.config.num_steps\\\\n        dt = -1.0 / num_steps\\\\n        dev = prefix_pad_masks.device\\\\n\\\\n        all_actions = []\\\\n        for _ in range(K):\\\\n            noise = self.flow.sample_noise((B, chunk_size, max_action_dim), dev)\\\\n            x_t = noise\\\\n            for step in range(num_steps):\\\\n                t = 1.0 + step * dt\\\\n                time_tensor = torch.tensor(t, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev).expand(B)\\\\n                v_t = self.flow.denoise_step(\\\\n                    x_t=x_t,\\\\n                    prefix_pad_masks=prefix_pad_masks,\\\\n                    past_key_values=past_key_values,\\\\n                    timestep=time_tensor,\\\\n                )\\\\n                x_t = x_t + dt * v_t\\\\n\\\\n            actions = x_t[:, :, :original_dim]\\\\n            actions = self._unnormalize_action(actions)\\\\n            all_actions.append(actions)\\\\n\\\\n        actions_K = torch.stack(all_actions, dim=1)  # (B, K, chunk_size, action_dim)\\\\n        return obs_features, actions_K\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef main():\\\\n    args = parse_args()\\\\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\\\\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\\\\n\\\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\\\n\\\\n    precomputer = LerobotNativePrecomputer(args.policy_path, device=args.device)\\\\n\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"Loading LIBERO {args.suite} envs...\\\\\\\")\\\\n    task_envs = load_libero_env(args.suite)\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"Loaded {len(task_envs)} tasks\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    max_steps = args.max_steps or SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 300)\\\\n\\\\n    all_obs_features = []\\\\n    all_actions_K = []\\\\n task_info[\\\\\\\"task_description\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        env = task_info[\\\\\\\"env\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        init_states = task_info[\\\\\\\"init_states\\\\\\\"]\\\\n        n_eps = min(args.n_episodes, len(init_states))\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\nTask {task_idx}: {task_desc} ({n_eps} eps, max_steps={max_steps})\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        for ep_idx in range(n_eps):\\\\n            env.reset()\\\\n            obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep_idx])\\\\n            precomputer.policy.reset()\\\\n\\\\n            ep_steps = 0\\\\n            for step in range(max_steps):\\\\n                batch = precomputer.build_batch(obs, task_desc)\\\\n                obs_feat, actions_K = precomputer.precompute_step(batch, K=args.K)\\\\n\\\\n                expert_action = actions_K[:, 0]  # first sample as expert\\\\n\\\\n                all_obs_features.append(obs_feat.cpu().squeeze(0))\\\\n                all_actions_K.append(actions_K.cpu().squeeze(0))\\\\n                all_expert_actions.append(expert_action.cpu().squeeze(0))\\\\n                total_steps += 1\\\\n                ep_steps += 1\\\\n\\\\n                # Execute first step of expert action chunk in env\\\\n                action_np = expert_action[0, 0].cpu().numpy()\\\\n                obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action_np)\\\\n\\\\n                if done:\\\\n                    break\\\\n\\\\n            success = info.get(\\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\", False) if info else False\\\\n            print(f\\\\\\\"  ep {ep_idx}: {ep_steps} steps, success={success}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        env.close()\\\\n\\\\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\\\\n\\\\n    obs_features_all = torch.stack(all_obs_features)\\\\n    actions_K_all = torch.stack(all_actions_K)\\\\n    expert_actions_all = torch.stack(all_expert_actions)\\\\n\\\\n    save_path = output_dir / \\\\\\\"precomputed.pt\\\\\\\"\\\\n    torch.save({\\\\n        \\\\\\\"obs_features\\\\\\\": obs_features_all,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"actions_K\\\\\\\": actions_K_all,\\\\n But I'm realizing I need to check what format robosuite's `agentview_image` is in and whether it matches what lerobot expects.\n\nActually, I just caught something important about image orientation. The original LIBERO evaluation code flips images vertically to account for this. If the lerobot training data was collected with this flip applied but the precompute script doesn't apply it, the model will see images in the wrong orientation.\n\nThe script comments claim \"lerobot doesn't flip,\" but I need to verify this. In lerobot's SmolVLA, calling `reset()` at the start of each episode should be fine, but there's a subtle difference in how actions are generated. During normal eval, `select_action` maintains an action queue—it generates a chunk of actions and pops from the queue on subsequent calls until generating a fresh chunk. The precompute script bypasses this by calling `precompute_step` directly at every step, which means each step gets an independent action prediction rather than benefiting from temporal consistency. self._env.set_init_state(self._init_states[self.init_state_id % len(self._init_states)])\\n            self.init_state_id += self._reset_stride  # Change init_state_id when reset\\n\\n        # After reset, objects may be unstable (slightly floating, intersecting, etc.).\\n        # Step the simulator with a no-op action for a few frames so everything settles.\\n        # Increasing this value can improve determinism and reproducibility across resets.\\n        for _ in range(self.num_steps_wait):\\n            raw_obs, _, _, _ = self._env.step(get_libero_dummy_action())\\n\\n        if self.control_mode == \\\"absolute\\\":\\n            for robot in self._env.robots:\\n                robot.controller.use_delta = False\\n        elif self.control_mode == \\\"relative\\\":\\n            for robot in self._env.robots:\\n                robot.controller.use_delta = True\\n        else:\\n            raise ValueError(f\\\"Invalid control mode: {self.control_mode}\\\")\\n        observation = self._format_raw_obs(raw_obs)\\n        info = {\\\"is_success\\\": False}\\n        return observation, info\\n\\n    def step(self, action: np.ndarray) -> tuple[RobotObservation, float, bool, bool, dict[str, Any]]:\\n        self._ensure_env()\\n        assert self._env is not None\\n        if action.ndim != 1:\\n            raise ValueError(\\n                f\\\"Expected action to be 1-D (shape (action_dim,)), \\\"\\n                f\\\"but got shape {action.shape} with ndim={action.ndim}\\\"\\n            )\\n        raw_obs, reward, done, info = self._env.step(action)\\n\\n        is_success = self._env.check_success()\\n        terminated = done or is_success\\n        info.update(\\n            {\\n                \\\"task\\\": self.task,\\n                \\\"task_id\\\": self.task_id,\\n                \\\"done\\\": done,\\n                \\\"is_success\\\": is_success,\\n            }\\n        )\\n        observation = self._format_raw_obs(raw_obs)\\n        if terminated:\\n            self.reset()\\n        truncated = False\\n        return observation, reward, terminated, truncated, info\\n\\n    def close(self):\\n        if self._env is not None:\\n            self._env.close()\\n\\n\\ndef _make_env_fns(\\n    *,\\n    suite,\\n    suite_name: str,\\n    task_id: int,\\n    n_envs: int,\\n    camera_names: list[str],\\n    episode_length: int | None,\\n    init_states: bool,\\n    gym_kwargs: Mapping[str, Any],\\n    control_mode: str,\\n    camera_name_mapping: dict[str, str] | None = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_012KVcwYPKmhPaUjYa6q1EoZ","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"#!/usr/bin/env python\\\\n\\\\n# Copyright 2024 The HuggingFace Inc. team. All rights reserved.\\\\n#\\\\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \\\\\\\"License\\\\\\\");\\\\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\\\\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\\\\n#\\\\n#     http://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\\\\n#\\\\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\\\\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \\\\\\\"AS IS\\\\\\\" BASIS,\\\\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\\\\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\\\\n# limitations under the License.\\\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\\\n\\\\nfrom typing import Any\\\\n\\\\nimport gymnasium as gym\\\\n\\\\nfrom .configs import EnvConfig, HubEnvConfig\\\\nfrom .utils import _call_make_env, _download_hub_file, _import_hub_module, _normalize_hub_result\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_env_config(env_type: str, **kwargs) -> EnvConfig:\\\\n    try:\\\\n        cls = EnvConfig.get_choice_class(env_type)\\\\n    except KeyError as err:\\\\n        raise ValueError(\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"Environment type '{env_type}' is not registered. \\\\\\\"\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"Available: {list(EnvConfig.get_known_choices().keys())}\\\\\\\"\\\\n        ) from err\\\\n    return cls(**kwargs)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_env_pre_post_processors(\\\\n    env_cfg: EnvConfig,\\\\n    policy_cfg: Any,\\\\n) -> tuple[Any, Any]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Create preprocessor and postprocessor pipelines for environment observations.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns a tuple of (preprocessor, postprocessor). By default, delegates to\\\\n    ``env_cfg.get_env_processors()``.  The XVLAConfig policy-specific override\\\\n    stays here because it depends on the *policy* config, not the env config.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    from lerobot.policies.xvla.configuration_xvla import XVLAConfig\\\\n\\\\n    if isinstance(policy_cfg, XVLAConfig):\\\\n        from lerobot.policies.xvla.processor_xvla import make_xvla_libero_pre_post_processors\\\\n\\\\n        return make_xvla_libero_pre_post_processors()\\\\n\\\\n    return env_cfg.get_env_processors()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef make_env(\\\\n    cfg: EnvConfig | str,\\\\n    n_envs: int = 1,\\\\n    use_async_envs: bool = False,\\\\n    hub_cache_dir: str | None = None,\\\\n    trust_remote_code: bool = False,\\\\n) -> dict[str, dict[int, gym.vector.VectorEnv]]:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Makes a gym vector environment according to the config or Hub reference.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        cfg (EnvConfig | str): Either an `EnvConfig` object describing the environment to build locally,\\\\n            or a Hugging Face Hub repository identifier (e.g. `\\\\\\\"username/repo\\\\\\\"`). In the latter case,\\\\n            the repo must include a Python file (usually `env.py`).\\\\n        n_envs (int, optional): The number of parallelized env to return. Defaults to 1.\\\\n        use_async_envs (bool, optional): Whether to return an AsyncVectorEnv or a SyncVectorEnv. Defaults to\\\\n            False.\\\\n        hub_cache_dir (str | None): Optional cache path for downloaded hub files.\\\\n        trust_remote_code (bool): **Explicit consent** to execute remote code from the Hub.\\\\n            Default False — must be set to True to import/exec hub `env.py`.\\\\n    Raises:\\\\n        ValueError: if n_envs < 1\\\\n        ModuleNotFoundError: If the requested env package is not installed\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        dict[str, dict[int, gym.vector.VectorEnv]]:\\\\n            A mapping from suite name to indexed vectorized environments.\\\\n            - For multi-task benchmarks (e.g., LIBERO): one entry per suite, and one vec env per task_id.\\\\n            - For single-task environments: a single suite entry (cfg.type) with task_id=0.\\\\n\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    # if user passed a hub id string (e.g., \\\\\\\"username/repo\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"username/repo@main:env.py\\\\\\\")\\\\n    # simplified: only support hub-provided `make_env`\\\\n    # TODO: (jadechoghari): deprecate string API and remove this check\\\\n    if isinstance(cfg, str):\\\\n        hub_path: str | None = cfg\\\\n    elif isinstance(cfg, HubEnvConfig):\\\\n        hub_path = cfg.hub_path\\\\n    else:\\\\n        hub_path = None\\\\n\\\\n    # If hub_path is set, download and call hub-provided `make_env`\\\\n    if hub_path:\\\\n        # _download_hub_file will raise the same RuntimeError if trust_remote_code is False\\\\n        repo_id, file_path, local_file, revision = _download_hub_file(\\\\n            hub_path, trust_remote_code, hub_cache_dir\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        # import and surface clear import errors\\\\n        module = _import_hub_module(local_file, repo_id)\\\\n\\\\n        # call the hub-provided make_env\\\\n        env_cfg = None if isinstance(cfg, str) else cfg\\\\n        raw_result = _call_make_env(module, n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs, cfg=env_cfg)\\\\n\\\\n        # normalize the return into {suite: {task_id: vec_env}}\\\\n        return _normalize_hub_result(raw_result)\\\\n\\\\n    # At this point, cfg must be an EnvConfig (not a string) since hub_path would have been set otherwise\\\\n    if isinstance(cfg, str):\\\\n        raise TypeError(\\\\\\\"cfg should be an EnvConfig at this point\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    if n_envs < 1:\\\\n        raise ValueError(\\\\\\\"`n_envs` must be at least 1\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    return cfg.create_envs(n_envs=n_envs, use_async_envs=use_async_envs)\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.35}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2e5005b7-4383-4494-8f3b-f04ce9f94618","uuid":"7e9bc600-b2e5-4758-99d2-f20eb32e03b2","timestamp":"2026-05-22T16:34:08.494Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"#!/usr/bin/env python\\\\n\\\\n# Copyright 2024 The HuggingFace Inc. team. Defaults to\\n            False.\\n        hub_cache_dir (str | None): Optional cache path for downloaded hub files.\\n        trust_remote_code (bool): **Explicit consent** to execute remote code from the Hub.\\n            Default False — must be set to True to import/exec hub `env.py`.\\n    Raises:\\n        ValueError: if n_envs < 1\\n        ModuleNotFoundError: If the requested env package is not installed\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        dict[str, dict[int, gym.vector.VectorEnv]]:\\n            A mapping from suite name to indexed vectorized environments.\\n            - eef_quat = robot_state[\\\\\\\"eef\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"quat\\\\\\\"]  # (B, 4,)\\\\n            gripper_qpos = robot_state[\\\\\\\"gripper\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"qpos\\\\\\\"]  # (B, 2,)\\\\n\\\\n            # Convert quaternion to axis-angle\\\\n            eef_axisangle = self._quat2axisangle(eef_quat)  # (B, 3)\\\\n            # Concatenate into a single state vector\\\\n            state = torch.cat((eef_pos, eef_axisangle, gripper_qpos), dim=-1)\\\\n\\\\n            # ensure float32\\\\n            state = state.float()\\\\n            if state.dim() == 1:\\\\n                state = {}\\\\n\\\\n        # add our new flattened state\\\\n        state_feats[OBS_STATE] = PolicyFeature(\\\\n            type=FeatureType.STATE,\\\\n            shape=(8,),  # [eef_pos(3), axis_angle(3),       - Gripper joint positions (2D)\\n    -   Maps the concatenated state to `\\\"observation.state\\\"`.\\n\\n    **Image Processing:**\\n    -   Rotates images by 180 degrees by flipping both height and width dimensions.\\n    -   This accounts for the HuggingFaceVLA/libero camera orientation convention.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def _process_observation(self, observation):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Processes both image and robot_state observations from LIBERO.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        processed_obs = observation.copy()\\n        for key in list(processed_obs.keys()):\\n            if key.startswith(f\\\"{OBS_IMAGES}.\\\"):\\n                img = processed_obs[key]\\n\\n                # Flip both H and W\\n                img = torch.flip(img, dims=[2, 3])\\n\\n                processed_obs[key] = img\\n        # Process robot_state into a flat state vector\\n        observation_robot_state_str = OBS_PREFIX + \\\"robot_state\\\"\\n        if observation_robot_state_str in processed_obs:\\n            robot_state = processed_obs.pop(observation_robot_state_str)\\n\\n            # Extract components\\n            eef_pos = robot_state[\\\"eef\\\"][\\\"pos\\\"]  # (B, 3,)\\n            eef_quat = robot_state[\\\"eef\\\"][\\\"quat\\\"]  # (B, 4,)\\n            gripper_qpos = robot_state[\\\"gripper\\\"][\\\"qpos\\\"]  # (B, 2,)\\n\\n            # Convert quaternion to axis-angle\\n            eef_axisangle = self._quat2axisangle(eef_quat)  # (B, 3)\\n            # Concatenate into a single state vector\\n            state = torch.cat((eef_pos, eef_axisangle, gripper_qpos), dim=-1)\\n\\n            # ensure float32\\n            state = state.float()\\n            if state.dim() == 1:\\n                state = state.unsqueeze(0)\\n\\n            processed_obs[OBS_STATE] = state\\n        return processed_obs\\n\\n    def transform_features(\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Transforms feature keys from the LIBERO format to the LeRobot standard.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        new_features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]] = {}\\n\\n        # copy over non-STATE features\\n        for ft, feats in features.items():\\n            if ft != FeatureType.STATE:\\n                new_features[ft] = feats.copy()\\n\\n        # rebuild STATE features\\n        state_feats = {}\\n\\n        # add our new flattened state\\n        state_feats[OBS_STATE] = PolicyFeature(\\n            type=FeatureType.STATE,\\n            shape=(8,),  # [eef_pos(3), axis_angle(3), gripp\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 269 字符] ...\\n\\n   \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Convert batched quaternions to axis-angle format.\\n        Only accepts torch tensors of shape (B, 4).\\n\\n        Args:\\n            quat (Tensor): (B, 4) tensor of quaternions in (x, y, z, w) format\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Tensor: (B, 3) axis-angle vectors\\n\\n        Raises:\\n            TypeError: if input is not a torch tensor\\n            ValueError: if shape is not (B, 4)\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n        if not isinstance(quat, torch.Tensor):\\n            raise TypeError(f\\\"_quat2axisangle expected a torch.Tensor, got {type(quat)}\\\")\\n\\n        if quat.ndim != 2 or quat.shape[1] != 4:\\n            raise ValueError(f\\\"_quat2axisangle expected shape (B, 4), got {tuple(quat.shape)}\\\")\\n\\n        quat = quat.to(dtype=torch.float32)\\n        device = quat.device\\n        batch_size = quat.shape[0]\\n\\n        w = quat[:, 3].clamp(-1.0, 1.0)\\n\\n        den = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp(1.0 - w * w, min=0.0))\\n\\n        result = torch.zeros((batch_size, 3), device=device)\\n\\n        mask = den > 1e-10\\n\\n        if mask.any():\\n            angle = 2.0 * torch.acos(w[mask])  # (M,)\\n            axis = quat[mask, :3] / den[mask].unsqueeze(1)\\n            result[mask] = axis * angle.unsqueeze(1)\\n\\n        return result\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\n@ProcessorStepRegistry.register(name=\\\"isaaclab_arena_processor\\\")\\nclass IsaaclabArenaProcessorStep(ObservationProcessorStep):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Processes IsaacLab Arena observations into LeRobot format.\\n\\n    **State Processing:**\\n    - Extracts state components from obs[\\\"policy\\\"] based on `state_keys`.\\n    - Concatenates into a flat vector mapped to \\\"observation.state\\\".\\n\\n    **Image Processing:**\\n    - Extracts images from obs[\\\"camera_obs\\\"] based on `camera_keys`.\\n    - Converts from (B, H, W, C) uint8 to (B, C, H, W) float32 [0, 1].\\n    - Maps to \\\"observation.images.&lt;camera_name>\\\".\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    # Configurable from IsaacLabEnv config / cli args: --env.state_keys=\\\"robot_joint_pos,left_eef_pos\\\"\\n    state_keys: tuple[str, ...]\\n\\n    # Configurable from IsaacLabEnv config / cli args: --env.camera_keys=\\\"robot_pov_cam_rgb\\\"\\n    camera_keys: tuple[str, ...]\\n\\n    def _process_observation(self, observation):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Processes both image and policy state observations from IsaacLab Arena.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        processed_obs = {}\\n\\n        if f\\\"{OBS_STR}.camera_obs\\\" in observation:\\n            camera_obs = observation[f\\\"{OBS_STR}.camera_obs\\\"]\\n\\n            for cam_name, img in camera_obs.items():\\n                if cam_name not in self.camera_keys:\\n                    continue\\n\\n                img = img.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()\\n                if img.dtype == torch.uint8:\\n                    img = img.float() / 255.0\\n                elif img.dtype != torch.float32:\\n                    img = img.float()\\n\\n                processed_obs[f\\\"{OBS_IMAGES}.{cam_name}\\\"] = img\\n\\n        # Process policy state -> observation.state\\n        if f\\\"{OBS_STR}.policy\\\" in observation:\\n            policy_obs = observation[f\\\"{OBS_STR}.policy\\\"]\\n\\n            # Collect state components in order\\n            state_components = []\\n            for key in self.state_keys:\\n                if key in policy_obs:\\n                    component = policy_obs[key]\\n                    # Flatten extra dims: (B, N, M) -> (B, N*M)\\n                    if component.dim() > 2:\\n                        batch_size = component.shape[0]\\n                        component = component.view(batch_size, -1)\\n                    state_components.append(component)\\n\\n            if state_components:\\n                state = torch.cat(state_components, dim=-1)\\n                state = state.float()\\n                processed_obs[OBS_STATE] = state\\n\\n        return processed_obs\\n\\n    def transform_features(\\n        self, features: dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]\\n    ) -> dict[PipelineFeatureType, dict[str, PolicyFeature]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Not used for policy evaluation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return features\\n\\n    def observation(self, observation):\\n        return self._process_observation(observation)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.32}}"}}}

Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\\\n    2.  Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\\\n    3.  Adding a batch dimension.\\\\n    4.  Ensuring the language task description ends with a newline character.\\\\n    5.  Tokenizing the language task description.\\\\n    6.  Moving all data to the specified device.\\\\n\\\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\\\n    1.  Moving data to the CPU.\\\\n    2.  Unnormalizing the output actions to their original scale.\\\\n\\\\n    Args:\\\\n        config: The configuration object for the SmolVLA policy.\\\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\\\n\\\\n    Returns:\\\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    input_steps = [\\\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\\\n        NewLineTaskProcessorStep(),\\\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            tokenizer_name=config.vlm_model_name,\\\\n            padding=config.pad_language_to,\\\\n            padding_side=\\\\\\\"right\\\\\\\",\\\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\\\n        ),\\\\n    ]\\\\n    output_steps = [\\\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\\\n        ),\\\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\"),\\\\n    ]\\\\n    return (\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\\\n            steps=input_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n        ),\\\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\\\n            steps=output_steps,\\\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\\\n        ),\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.32}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2e5005b7-4383-4494-8f3b-f04ce9f94618","uuid":"ecd0e5ba-adb8-4c4b-83d9-07d61ac221f2","timestamp":"2026-05-22T16:35:22.313Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"#!/usr/bin/env python\\\\n\\\\n# Copyright 2025 HuggingFace Inc. team. Renaming features to match pretrained configurations.\\n    2.  Normalizing input and output features based on dataset statistics.\\n    3.  Adding a batch dimension.\\n    4.  Ensuring the language task description ends with a newline character.\\n    5.  Tokenizing the language task description.\\n    6.  Moving all data to the specified device.\\n\\n    The post-processing pipeline handles the model's output by:\\n    1.  Moving data to the CPU.\\n    2.  Unnormalizing the output actions to their original scale.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        config: The configuration object for the SmolVLA policy.\\n        dataset_stats: A dictionary of statistics for normalization.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        A tuple containing the configured pre-processor and post-processor pipelines.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    input_steps = [\\n        RenameObservationsProcessorStep(rename_map={}),  # To mimic the same processor as pretrained one\\n        AddBatchDimensionProcessorStep(),\\n        NewLineTaskProcessorStep(),\\n        TokenizerProcessorStep(\\n            tokenizer_name=config.vlm_model_name,\\n            padding=config.pad_language_to,\\n            padding_side=\\\"right\\\",\\n            max_length=config.tokenizer_max_length,\\n        ),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=config.device),\\n        NormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features={**config.input_features, **config.output_features},\\n            norm_map=config.normalization_mapping,\\n            stats=dataset_stats,\\n        ),\\n    ]\\n    output_steps = [\\n        UnnormalizerProcessorStep(\\n            features=config.output_features, norm_map=config.normalization_mapping, stats=dataset_stats\\n        ),\\n        DeviceProcessorStep(device=\\\"cpu\\\"),\\n    ]\\n    return (\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]](\\n            steps=input_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_PREPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n        ),\\n        PolicyProcessorPipeline[PolicyAction, PolicyAction](\\n            steps=output_steps,\\n            name=POLICY_POSTPROCESSOR_DEFAULT_NAME,\\n            to_transition=policy_action_to_transition,\\n            to_output=transition_to_policy_action,\\n        ),\\n    )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.32}}"}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MuDPfQ6h9yWdBe3fMvbnqe","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"import os\\\\r\\\\nimport xml.etree.ElementTree as ET\\\\r\\\\nfrom collections import OrderedDict\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\nimport robosuite\\\\r\\\\nimport robosuite.macros as macros\\\\r\\\\nfrom robosuite.models.base import MujocoModel\\\\r\\\\nfrom robosuite.renderers.base import load_renderer_config\\\\r\\\\nfrom robosuite.utils import OpenCVRenderer, SimulationError, XMLError\\\\r\\\\nfrom robosuite.utils.binding_utils import MjRenderContextOffscreen, MjSim\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\nREGISTERED_ENVS = {}\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\ndef register_env(target_class):\\\\r\\\\n    REGISTERED_ENVS[target_class.__name__] = target_class\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\ndef make(env_name, *args, **kwargs):\\\\r\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n    Instantiates a robosuite environment.\\\\r\\\\n    This method attempts to mirror the equivalent functionality of gym.make in a somewhat sloppy way.\\\\r\\\\n    Args:\\\\r\\\\n        env_name (str): Name of the robosuite environment to initialize\\\\r\\\\n        *args: Additional arguments to pass to the specific environment class initializer\\\\r\\\\n        **kwargs: Additional arguments to pass to the specific environment class initializer\\\\r\\\\n    Returns:\\\\r\\\\n        MujocoEnv: Desired robosuite environment\\\\r\\\\n    Raises:\\\\r\\\\n        Exception: [Invalid environment name]\\\\r\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n    if env_name not in REGISTERED_ENVS:\\\\r\\\\n        raise Exception(\\\\r\\\\n            \\\\\\\"Environment {} not found. Make sure it is a registered environment among: {}\\\\\\\".format(\\\\r\\\\n                env_name, \\\\\\\", \\\\\\\".join(REGISTERED_ENVS)\\\\r\\\\n            )\\\\r\\\\n        )\\\\r\\\\n    return REGISTERED_ENVS[env_name](*args, **kwargs)\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\nclass EnvMeta(type):\\\\r\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Metaclass for registering environments\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n    def __new__(meta, name, bases, class_dict):\\\\r\\\\n        cls = super().__new__(meta, name, bases, class_dict)\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n        # List all environments that should not be registered here.\\\\r\\\\n        _unregistered_envs = [\\\\\\\"MujocoEnv\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"RobotEnv\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"ManipulationEnv\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"SingleArmEnv\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"TwoArmEnv\\\\\\\"]\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n        if cls.__name__ not in _unregistered_envs:\\\\r\\\\n            register_env(cls)\\\\r\\\\n        return cls\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\n\\\\r\\\\nclass MujocoEnv(metaclass=EnvMeta):\\\\r\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\r\\\\n    Initializes a Mujoco Environment.\\\\r\\\\n    Args:\\\\r\\\\n        has_renderer (bool): If true, render the simulation state in\\\\r\\\\n            a viewer instead of headless mode.\\\\r\\\\n        has_offscreen_renderer (bool): True if using off-screen rendering.\\\\r\\\\n        render_camera (str): Name of camera to render if `has_renderer` is True. Setting this value to 'None'\\\\r\\\\n            will result in the default angle being applied, which is useful as it can be dragged / panned by\\\\r\\\\n            the user using the mouse\\\\r\\\\n        render_collision_mesh (bool): True if rendering collision meshes\\\\r\\\\n            in camera. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01PWen4XMkPVCxrtz8wj6nHn","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I have the full picture:\n\n### Root Causes\n\n**1. Missing image flip (180° rotation) — Primary cause of 0% SR**\n- lerobot's `LiberoProcessorStep` applies `torch.flip(img, dims=[2, 3])` to BOTH H and W dimensions\n- The precompute script passes raw images without this flip\n- The SmolVLA model was trained with 180°-rotated images, so feeding unrotated images produces garbage actions\n\n**2. Wrong success check — Causes false 0% SR reporting**\n- The script uses `info.get(\"success\", False)` \n\n\n- robosuite's base `_post_action` returns an empty info dict `{}`\n- Should use `env.check_success()` which delegates to `env.env._check_success()`\n\n**3. Data saving** \n- The script saves data only at the very end after ALL episodes complete\n- If the user kills the process after seeing 0% SR on the first episodes, no data is saved\n- This is a design issue, but fixing the above bugs should resolve the practical problem\n\n### Fix Plan\n\n1. In `build_batch()`, flip both images 180° after ToTensor + unsqueeze...\n\n2. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011LeVUWan2Xk8HNKVhNzt9e","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Lerobot-native precompute for ACR-VLA CRM training.\n2\t\n3\tUses lerobot's official SmolVLA inference path (same as eval), ensuring\n4\taction distribution consistency between precompute and eval.\n5\t\n6\tKey differences from SmolVLAWrapper-based precompute:\n7\t  - No image H/W flip (lerobot doesn't flip)\n8\t  - Uses lerobot's prepare_images / prepare_state pipeline directly\n9\t  - Language tokenization with smolvla_new_line_processor convention\n10\t  - Action unnormalization via dataset MEAN_STD stats\n11\t\n12\tModel is called only at decision points (every chunk_size=50 env steps),\n13\tnot at every env step. A 280-step episode has ~6 decision points.\n14\t\n15\tInput:  LIBERO env + SmolVLA policy (lerobot/smolvla_libero)\n16\tOutput: Per-decision-point .pt with {obs_features, actions_K, expert_action}\n17\t\"\"\"\n18\timport argparse\n19\timport functools\n20\tbuiltins_print = print\n21\tprint = functools.partial(builtins_print, flush=True)\n22\timport os\n23\tos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\n24\tos.environ.setdefault(\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\n25\timport sys\n26\timport time\n27\tfrom pathlib import Path\n28\t\n29\timport torch\n30\timport numpy as np\n31\tfrom torchvision import transforms as T\n32\tfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\n33\tfrom robosuite.utils.transform_utils import quat2axisangle\n34\t\n35\tfrom lerobot.policies.smolvla.modeling_smolvla import (\n36\t    SmolVLAPolicy,\n37\t    VLAFlowMatching,\n38\t    make_att_2d_masks,\n39\t)\n40\tfrom lerobot.utils.constants import OBS_STATE, OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS, OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK\n41\t\n42\tFULL_ACTION_DIM = 7\n43\tCORRECTION_DIM = 6\n44\t\n45\tSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\"object\": 280, \"spatial\": 280, \"goal\": 300, \"long\": 520, \"90\": 400}\n46\tBENCHMARK_MAP = {\n47\t    \"spatial\": \"libero_spatial\",\n48\t    \"object\": \"libero_object\",\n49\t    \"goal\": \"libero_goal\",\n50\t    \"long\": \"libero_10\",\n51\t    \"90\": \"libero_90\",\n52\t}\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef parse_args():\n56\t    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Lerobot-native precompute for CRM training\")\n57\t    p.add_argument(\"--policy_path\", type=str, default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero/\")\n58\t    p.add_argument(\"--suite\", \"--benchmark\", type=str, default=\"object\",\n59\t                   choices=[\"object\", \"spatial\", \"goal\", \"long\", \"90\"])\n60\t    p.add_argument(\"--K\", type=int, default=10)\n61\t    p.add_argument(\"--n_episodes\", type=int, default=10)\n62\t    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str, required=True)\n63\t    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n64\t    p.add_argument(\"--device\", type=str, default=\"cuda\")\n65\t    p.add_argument(\"--max_steps\", type=int, default=None,\n66\t                   help=\"Override max steps per episode (default: suite-specific)\")\n67\t    return p.parse_args()\n68\t\n69\t\n70\tdef get_robot_state(obs):\n71\t    \"\"\"8D robot state: eef_pos(3) + eef_axisangle(3) + gripper_qpos(2).\"\"\"\n72\t    eef_pos = obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"]\n73\t    eef_axisangle = quat2axisangle(obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"])\n74\t    gripper_qpos = obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"]\n75\t    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_axisangle, gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float64)\n76\t\n77\t\n78\tdef load_libero_env(suite_name):\n79\t    from libero.libero import benchmark as bm\n80\t    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n81\t\n82\t    bm_name = BENCHMARK_MAP[suite_name]\n83\t    benchmark_instance = bm.get_benchmark(bm_name)()\n84\t\n85\t    task_envs = []\n86\t    for task_idx in range(benchmark_instance.n_tasks):\n87\t        task = benchmark_instance.get_task(task_idx)\n88\t        task_bddl = benchmark_instance.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_idx)\n89\t        env_args = {\n90\t            \"bddl_file_name\": task_bddl,\n91\t            \"camera_heights\": 256,\n92\t            \"camera_widths\": 256,\n93\t            \"camera_names\": [\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n94\t            \"render_gpu_device_id\": int(os.environ.get(\"MUJOCO_EGL_DEVICE_ID\", \"0\")),\n95\t        }\n96\t        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n97\t        env.seed(42)\n98\t        task_envs.append({\n99\t            \"task_name\": task.name,\n100\t            \"task_description\": task.language,\n101\t            \"env\": env,\n102\t            \"init_states\": benchmark_instance.get_task_init_states(task_idx),\n103\t        })\n104\t    return task_envs\n105\t\n106\t\n107\tclass LerobotNativePrecomputer:\n108\t    \"\"\"Precompute using lerobot's official SmolVLA inference path.\n109\t\n110\t    Replicates the exact preprocessing pipeline from lerobot eval:\n111\t      1. Images: ToTensor [0,1] → prepare_images (resize_with_pad + normalize [-1,1])\n112\t      2. State: 8D → MEAN_STD normalize → prepare_state (pad to 32D)\n113\t      3. Language: append \\\\n → tokenize (max_length=48, padding=max_length)\n114\t    Then uses VLAFlowMatching.sample_actions for action generation (same as\n115\t    SmolVLAPolicy._get_action_chunk → model.sample_actions).\n116\t    \"\"\"\n117\t\n118\t    def __init__(self, policy_path, device=\"cuda\"):\n119\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n120\t\n121\t        print(f\"Loading SmolVLA from {policy_path}...\")\n122\t        self.policy = SmolVLAPolicy.from_pretrained(policy_path)\n123\t        self.policy.to(self.device)\n124\t        self.policy.eval()\n125\t        for p in self.policy.parameters():\n126\t            p.requires_grad = False\n127\t\n128\t        self.flow: VLAFlowMatching = self.policy.model\n129\t        self.config = self.policy.config\n130\t        self.processor = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.processor\n131\t        self.tokenizer = self.processor.tokenizer\n132\t\n133\t        model_dir = Path(policy_path)\n134\t        pre_stats = load_file(str(model_dir / \"policy_preprocessor_step_5_normalizer_processor.safetensors\"))\n135\t        post_stats = load_file(str(model_dir / \"policy_postprocessor_step_0_unnormalizer_processor.safetensors\"))\n136\t\n137\t        self.state_mean = pre_stats[\"observation.state.mean\"].to(self.device)\n138\t        self.state_std = pre_stats[\"observation.state.std\"].to(self.device)\n139\t        self.action_mean = post_stats[\"action.mean\"].to(self.device)\n140\t        self.action_std = post_stats[\"action.std\"].to(self.device)\n141\t\n142\t        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.config.text_config.hidden_size\n143\t        self._to_tensor = T.ToTensor()\n144\t\n145\t        print(f\"  hidden_size={self.vlm_hidden_size}, \"\n146\t              f\"expert_hidden_size={self.flow.vlm_with_expert.expert_hidden_size}, \"\n147\t              f\"chunk_size={self.config.chunk_size}, \"\n148\t              f\"action_dim={self.config.action_feature.shape[0]}\")\n149\t\n150\t    def _normalize_state(self, state_raw):\n151\t        D = state_raw.shape[-1]\n152\t        return (state_raw - self.state_mean[:D]) / (self.state_std[:D] + 1e-8)\n153\t\n154\t    def _unnormalize_action(self, action_norm):\n155\t        return action_norm * self.action_std + self.action_mean\n156\t\n157\t    @torch.no_grad()\n158\t    def build_batch(self, obs, task_description):\n159\t        \"\"\"Convert raw LIBERO obs → lerobot batch dict.\n160\t\n161\t        Matches the lerobot preprocessor pipeline exactly:\n162\t          rename_observations → to_batch → smolvla_new_line → tokenizer → device → normalizer\n163\t        \"\"\"\n164\t        dev = self.device\n165\t        batch = {}\n166\t\n167\t        # Images: HWC uint8 → CHW float [0,1] — no flip (lerobot doesn't flip)\n168\t        agentview = self._to_tensor(obs[\"agentview_image\"]).unsqueeze(0).to(dev)\n169\t        batch[\"observation.images.camera1\"] = agentview\n170\t\n171\t        eye_in_hand = self._to_tensor(obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]).unsqueeze(0).to(dev)\n172\t        batch[\"observation.images.camera2\"] = eye_in_hand\n173\t\n174\t        # State: 8D → MEAN_STD normalize\n175\t        robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\n176\t        state_t = torch.tensor(robot_state, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)\n177\t        state_norm = self._normalize_state(state_t)\n178\t        batch[OBS_STATE] = state_norm.unsqueeze(0)\n179\t\n180\t        # Language: append \\n then tokenize (matches smolvla_new_line_processor + tokenizer_processor)\n181\t        task_text = task_description if task_description.endswith(\"\\n\") else f\"{task_description}\\n\"\n182\t        tokens = self.tokenizer(\n183\t            task_text,\n184\t            return_tensors=\"pt\",\n185\t            padding=\"max_length\",\n186\t            max_length=self.config.tokenizer_max_length,\n187\t            truncation=True,\n188\t        )\n189\t        batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS] = tokens[\"input_ids\"].to(dev)\n190\t        batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK] = tokens[\"attention_mask\"].to(dev).bool()\n191\t\n192\t        return batch\n193\t\n194\t    @torch.no_grad()\n195\t    def precompute_step(self, batch, K):\n196\t        \"\"\"One precompute step: obs → (obs_features, K action chunks).\n197\t\n198\t        Uses lerobot's prepare_images + prepare_state, then:\n199\t          1. embed_prefix → VLM forward → obs_features (mean-pooled) + KV cache\n200\t          2. K × denoise loop with fresh noise → K unnormalized action chunks\n201\t\n202\t        The KV cache stores the deterministic prefix representation. Only the\n203\t        action denoising noise varies across K samples, matching model.sample_actions\n204\t        semantics exactly.\n205\t        \"\"\"\n206\t        images, img_masks = self.policy.prepare_images(batch)\n207\t        state = self.policy.prepare_state(batch)\n208\t        lang_tokens = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS]\n209\t        lang_masks = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK]\n210\t\n211\t        # --- prefix encoding (run once) ---\n212\t        prefix_embs, prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks = self.flow.embed_prefix(\n213\t            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state=state\n214\t        )\n215\t        prefix_att_2d_masks = make_att_2d_masks(prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks)\n216\t        prefix_position_ids = torch.cumsum(prefix_pad_masks, dim=1) - 1\n217\t\n218\t        outputs_embeds, past_key_values = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.forward(\n219\t            attention_mask=prefix_att_2d_masks,\n220\t            position_ids=prefix_position_ids,\n221\t            past_key_values=None,\n222\t            inputs_embeds=[prefix_embs, None],\n223\t            use_cache=self.config.use_cache,\n224\t            fill_kv_cache=True,\n225\t        )\n226\t\n227\t        # obs_features: mean-pool VLM hidden states over valid prefix tokens\n228\t        vlm_hidden = outputs_embeds[0]  # (B, seq_len, hidden_size)\n229\t        mask = prefix_pad_masks.unsqueeze(-1).float()\n230\t        obs_features = (vlm_hidden.float() * mask).sum(1) / mask.sum(1).clamp(min=1)\n231\t\n232\t        # --- K action samples via cached prefix ---\n233\t        B = prefix_pad_masks.shape[0]\n234\t        chunk_size = self.config.chunk_size\n235\t        max_action_dim = self.config.max_action_dim\n236\t        original_dim = self.config.action_feature.shape[0]\n237\t        num_steps = self.config.num_steps\n238\t        dt = -1.0 / num_steps\n239\t        dev = prefix_pad_masks.device\n240\t\n241\t        all_actions = []\n242\t        for _ in range(K):\n243\t            noise = self.flow.sample_noise((B, chunk_size, max_action_dim), dev)\n244\t            x_t = noise\n245\t            for step in range(num_steps):\n246\t                t = 1.0 + step * dt\n247\t                time_tensor = torch.tensor(t, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev).expand(B)\n248\t                v_t = self.flow.denoise_step(\n249\t                    x_t=x_t,\n250\t                    prefix_pad_masks=prefix_pad_masks,\n251\t                    past_key_values=past_key_values,\n252\t                    timestep=time_tensor,\n253\t                )\n254\t                x_t = x_t + dt * v_t\n255\t\n256\t            actions = x_t[:, :, :original_dim]\n257\t            actions = self._unnormalize_action(actions)\n258\t            all_actions.append(actions)\n259\t\n260\t        actions_K = torch.stack(all_actions, dim=1)  # (B, K, chunk_size, action_dim)\n261\t        return obs_features, actions_K\n262\t\n263\t\n264\tdef main():\n265\t    args = parse_args()\n266\t    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n267\t    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n268\t\n269\t    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n270\t    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n271\t\n272\t    precomputer = LerobotNativePrecomputer(args.policy_path, device=args.device)\n273\t\n274\t    print(f\"Loading LIBERO {args.suite} envs...\")\n275\t    task_envs = load_libero_env(args.suite)\n276\t    print(f\"Loaded {len(task_envs)} tasks\")\n277\t\n278\t    max_steps = args.max_steps or SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 300)\n279\t\n280\t    all_obs_features = []\n281\t    all_actions_K = []\n282\t    all_expert_actions = []\n283\t    total_decision_points = 0\n284\t    total_env_steps = 0\n285\t    t0 = time.time()\n286\t    chunk_size = precomputer.config.chunk_size\n287\t\n288\t    for task_idx, task_info in enumerate(task_envs):\n289\t        task_desc = task_info[\"task_description\"]\n290\t        env = task_info[\"env\"]\n291\t        init_states = task_info[\"init_states\"]\n292\t        n_eps = min(args.n_episodes, len(init_states))\n293\t        print(f\"\\nTask {task_idx}: {task_desc} ({n_eps} eps, max_steps={max_steps}, chunk_size={chunk_size})\")\n294\t\n295\t        for ep_idx in range(n_eps):\n296\t            env.reset()\n297\t            obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep_idx])\n298\t            precomputer.policy.reset()\n299\t\n300\t            ep_env_steps = 0\n301\t            ep_decision_points = 0\n302\t            done = False\n303\t            ep_t0 = time.time()\n304\t\n305\t            while ep_env_steps < max_steps and not done:\n306\t                batch = precomputer.build_batch(obs, task_desc)\n307\t                obs_feat, actions_K = precomputer.precompute_step(batch, K=args.K)\n308\t                expert_chunk = actions_K[:, 0]  # (1, chunk_size, action_dim)\n309\t\n310\t                all_obs_features.append(obs_feat.cpu().squeeze(0))\n311\t                all_actions_K.append(actions_K.cpu().squeeze(0))\n312\t                all_expert_actions.append(expert_chunk.cpu().squeeze(0))\n313\t                ep_decision_points += 1\n314\t                total_decision_points += 1\n315\t\n316\t                for cs in range(chunk_size):\n317\t                    if ep_env_steps >= max_steps:\n318\t                        break\n319\t                    action_np = expert_chunk[0, cs].cpu().numpy()\n320\t precompute for ACR-VLA CRM training.\n\nUses lerobot's official SmolVLA inference path (same as eval), ensuring\naction distribution consistency between precompute and eval.\n\nKey differences from SmolVLAWrapper-based precompute:\n  - No image H/W flip (lerobot doesn't flip)\n  - Uses lerobot's prepare_images / prepare_state pipeline directly\n  - Language tokenization with smolvla_new_line_processor convention\n  - Action unnormalization via dataset MEAN_STD stats\n\nModel is called only at decision points (every chunk_size=50 env steps),\nnot at every env step. A 280-step episode has ~6 decision points.\n\nInput:  LIBERO env + SmolVLA policy (lerobot/smolvla_libero)\nOutput: Per-decision-point .pt with {obs_features, actions_K, expert_action}\n\"\"\"\nimport argparse\nimport functools\nbuiltins_print = print\nprint = functools.partial(builtins_print, flush=True)\nimport os\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE\", \"1\")\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\nimport numpy as np\nfrom torchvision import transforms as T\nfrom safetensors.torch import load_file\nfrom robosuite.utils.transform_utils import quat2axisangle\n\nfrom lerobot.policies.smolvla.modeling_smolvla import (\n    SmolVLAPolicy,\n    VLAFlowMatching,\n    make_att_2d_masks,\n)\nfrom lerobot.utils.constants import OBS_STATE, OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS, OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK\n\nFULL_ACTION_DIM = 7\nCORRECTION_DIM = 6\n\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\"object\": 280, \"spatial\": 280, \"goal\": 300, \"long\": 520, \"90\": 400}\nBENCHMARK_MAP = {\n    \"spatial\": \"libero_spatial\",\n    \"object\": \"libero_object\",\n    \"goal\": \"libero_goal\",\n    \"long\": \"libero_10\",\n    \"90\": \"libero_90\",\n}\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Lerobot-native precompute for CRM training\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--policy_path\", type=str, default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/smolvla_libero/\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--suite\", \"--benchmark\", type=str, default=\"object\",\n                   choices=[\"object\", \"spatial\", \"goal\", \"long\", \"90\"])\n    p.add_argument(\"--K\", type=int, default=10)\n    p.add_argument(\"--n_episodes\", type=int, default=10)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", type=str, required=True)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--device\", type=str, default=\"cuda\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--max_steps\", type=int, default=None,\n                   help=\"Override max steps per episode (default: suite-specific)\")\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\ndef get_robot_state(obs):\n    \"\"\"8D robot state: eef_pos(3) + eef_axisangle(3) + gripper_qpos(2).\"\"\"\n    eef_pos = obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"]\n    eef_axisangle = quat2axisangle(obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"])\n    gripper_qpos = obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"]\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_axisangle, gripper_qpos]).astype(np.float64)\n\n\ndef load_libero_env(suite_name):\n    from libero.libero import benchmark as bm\n    from libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\n    bm_name = BENCHMARK_MAP[suite_name]\n    benchmark_instance = bm.get_benchmark(bm_name)()\n\n    task_envs = []\n    for task_idx in range(benchmark_instance.n_tasks):\n        task = benchmark_instance.get_task(task_idx)\n        task_bddl = benchmark_instance.get_task_bddl_file_path(task_idx)\n        env_args = {\n            \"bddl_file_name\": task_bddl,\n            \"camera_heights\": 256,\n            \"camera_widths\": 256,\n            \"camera_names\": [\"agentview\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand\"],\n            \"render_gpu_device_id\": int(os.environ.get(\"MUJOCO_EGL_DEVICE_ID\", \"0\")),\n        }\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n        env.seed(42)\n        task_envs.append({\n            \"task_name\": task.name,\n            \"task_description\": task.language,\n            \"env\": env,\n            \"init_states\": benchmark_instance.get_task_init_states(task_idx),\n        })\n    return task_envs\n\n\nclass LerobotNativePrecomputer:\n    \"\"\"Precompute using lerobot's official SmolVLA inference path.\n\n    Replicates the exact preprocessing pipeline from lerobot eval:\n      1. Images: ToTensor [0,1] → prepare_images (resize_with_pad + normalize [-1,1])\n      2. State: 8D → MEAN_STD normalize → prepare_state (pad to 32D)\n      3. Language: append \\\\n → tokenize (max_length=48, padding=max_length)\n    Then uses VLAFlowMatching.sample_actions for action generation (same as\n    SmolVLAPolicy._get_action_chunk → model.sample_actions).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, policy_path, device=\"cuda\"):\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\n\n        print(f\"Loading SmolVLA from {policy_path}...\")\n        self.policy = SmolVLAPolicy.from_pretrained(policy_path)\n        self.policy.to(self.device)\n        self.policy.eval()\n        for p in self.policy.parameters():\n            p.requires_grad = False\n\n        self.flow: VLAFlowMatching = self.policy.model\n        self.config = self.policy.config\n        self.processor = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.processor\n        self.tokenizer = self.processor.tokenizer\n\n        model_dir = Path(policy_path)\n        pre_stats = load_file(str(model_dir / \"policy_preprocessor_step_5_normalizer_processor.safetensors\"))\n        post_stats = load_file(str(model_dir / \"policy_postprocessor_step_0_unnormalizer_processor.safetensors\"))\n\n        self.state_mean = pre_stats[\"observation.state.mean\"].to(self.device)\n        self.state_std = pre_stats[\"observation.state.std\"].to(self.device)\n        self.action_mean = post_stats[\"action.mean\"].to(self.device)\n        self.action_std = post_stats[\"action.std\"].to(self.device)\n\n        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.config.text_config.hidden_size\n        self._to_tensor = T.ToTensor()\n\n        print(f\"  hidden_size={self.vlm_hidden_size}, \"\n              f\"expert_hidden_size={self.flow.vlm_with_expert.expert_hidden_size}, \"\n              f\"chunk_size={self.config.chunk_size}, \"\n              f\"action_dim={self.config.action_feature.shape[0]}\")\n\n    def _normalize_state(self, state_raw):\n        D = state_raw.shape[-1]\n        return (state_raw - self.state_mean[:D]) / (self.state_std[:D] + 1e-8)\n\n    def _unnormalize_action(self, action_norm):\n        return action_norm * self.action_std + self.action_mean\n\n    @torch.no_grad()\n    def build_batch(self, obs, task_description):\n        \"\"\"Convert raw LIBERO obs → lerobot batch dict.\n\n        Matches the lerobot preprocessor pipeline exactly:\n          rename_observations → to_batch → smolvla_new_line → tokenizer → device → normalizer\n        \"\"\"\n        dev = self.device\n        batch = {}\n\n        # Images: HWC uint8 → CHW float [0,1] — no flip (lerobot doesn't flip)\n        agentview = self._to_tensor(obs[\"agentview_image\"]).unsqueeze(0).to(dev)\n        batch[\"observation.images.camera1\"] = agentview\n\n        eye_in_hand = self._to_tensor(obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]).unsqueeze(0).to(dev)\n        batch[\"observation.images.camera2\"] = eye_in_hand\n\n        # State: 8D → MEAN_STD normalize\n        robot_state = get_robot_state(obs)\n        state_t = torch.tensor(robot_state, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)\n        state_norm = self._normalize_state(state_t)\n        batch[OBS_STATE] = state_norm.unsqueeze(0)\n\n        # Language: append \\n then tokenize (matches smolvla_new_line_processor + tokenizer_processor)\n        task_text = task_description if task_description.endswith(\"\\n\") else f\"{task_description}\\n\"\n        tokens = self.tokenizer(\n            task_text,\n            return_tensors=\"pt\",\n            padding=\"max_length\",\n            max_length=self.config.tokenizer_max_length,\n            truncation=True,\n        )\n        batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS] = tokens[\"input_ids\"].to(dev)\n        batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK] = tokens[\"attention_mask\"].to(dev).bool()\n\n        return batch\n\n    @torch.no_grad()\n    def precompute_step(self, batch, K):\n        \"\"\"One precompute step: obs → (obs_features, K action chunks).\n\n        Uses lerobot's prepare_images + prepare_state, then:\n          1. embed_prefix → VLM forward → obs_features (mean-pooled) + KV cache\n          2. K × denoise loop with fresh noise → K unnormalized action chunks\n\n        The KV cache stores the deterministic prefix representation. Only the\n        action denoising noise varies across K samples, matching model.sample_actions\n        semantics exactly.\n        \"\"\"\n        images, img_masks = self.policy.prepare_images(batch)\n        state = self.policy.prepare_state(batch)\n        lang_tokens = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_TOKENS]\n        lang_masks = batch[OBS_LANGUAGE_ATTENTION_MASK]\n\n        # --- prefix encoding (run once) ---\n        prefix_embs, prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks = self.flow.embed_prefix(\n            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state=state\n        )\n        prefix_att_2d_masks = make_att_2d_masks(prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks)\n        prefix_position_ids = torch.cumsum(prefix_pad_masks, dim=1) - 1\n\n        outputs_embeds, past_key_values = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.forward(\n            attention_mask=prefix_att_2d_masks,\n            position_ids=prefix_position_ids,\n            past_key_values=None,\n            inputs_embeds=[prefix_embs, None],\n            use_cache=self.config.use_cache,\n            fill_kv_cache=True,\n        )\n\n        # obs_features: mean-pool VLM hidden states over valid prefix tokens\n        vlm_hidden = outputs_embeds[0]  # (B, seq_len, hidden_size)\n        mask = prefix_pad_masks.unsqueeze(-1).float()\n        obs_features = (vlm_hidden.float() * mask).sum(1) / mask.sum(1).clamp(min=1)\n\n        # --- K action samples via cached prefix ---\n        B = prefix_pad_masks.shape[0]\n        chunk_size = self.config.chunk_size\n        max_action_dim = self.config.max_action_dim\n        original_dim = self.config.action_feature.shape[0]\n        num_steps = self.config.num_steps\n        dt = -1.0 / num_steps\n        dev = prefix_pad_masks.device\n\n        all_actions = []\n        for _ in range(K):\n            noise = self.flow.sample_noise((B, chunk_size, max_action_dim), dev)\n            x_t = noise\n            for step in range(num_steps):\n                t = 1.0 + step * dt\n                time_tensor = torch.tensor(t, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev).expand(B)\n                v_t = self.flow.denoise_step(\n                    x_t=x_t,\n                    prefix_pad_masks=prefix_pad_masks,\n                    past_key_values=past_key_values,\n                    timestep=time_tensor,\n                )\n                x_t = x_t + dt * v_t\n\n            actions = x_t[:, :, :original_dim]\n            actions = self._unnormalize_action(actions)\n            all_actions.append(actions)\n\n        actions_K = torch.stack(all_actions, dim=1)  # (B, K, chunk_size, action_dim)\n        return obs_features, actions_K\n\n\ndef main():\n    args = parse_args()\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    precomputer = LerobotNativePrecomputer(args.policy_path, device=args.device)\n\n    print(f\"Loading LIBERO {args.suite} envs...\")\n    task_envs = load_libero_env(args.suite)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(task_envs)} tasks\")\n\n    max_steps = args.max_steps or SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 300)\n\n    all_obs_features = []\n    all_actions_K = []\n    all_expert_actions = []\n    total_decision_points = 0\n    total_env_steps = 0\n    t0 = time.time()\n    chunk_size = precomputer.config.chunk_size\n\n    for task_idx, task_info in enumerate(task_envs):\n        task_desc = task_info[\"task_description\"]\n        env = task_info[\"env\"]\n        init_states = task_info[\"init_states\"]\n        n_eps = min(args.n_episodes, len(init_states))\n        print(f\"\\nTask {task_idx}: {task_desc} ({n_eps} eps, max_steps={max_steps}, chunk_size={chunk_size})\")\n\n        for ep_idx in range(n_eps):\n            env.reset()\n            obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep_idx])\n            precomputer.policy.reset()\n\n            ep_env_steps = 0\n            ep_decision_points = 0\n            done = False\n            ep_t0 = time.time()\n\n            while ep_env_steps < max_steps and not done:\n                batch = precomputer.build_batch(obs, task_desc)\n                obs_feat, actions_K = precomputer.precompute_step(batch, K=args.K)\n                expert_chunk = actions_K[:, 0]  # (1, chunk_size, action_dim)\n\n                all_obs_features.append(obs_feat.cpu().squeeze(0))\n                all_actions_K.append(actions_K.cpu().squeeze(0))\n                all_expert_actions.append(expert_chunk.cpu().squeeze(0))\n                ep_decision_points += 1\n                total_decision_points += 1\n\n                for cs in range(chunk_size):\n                    if ep_env_steps >= max_steps:\n                        break\n                    action_np = expert_chunk[0, cs].cpu().numpy()\n                    obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action_np)\n                    ep_env_steps += 1\n                    if done:\n                        break\n\n            total_env_steps += ep_env_steps\n            success = info.get(\"success\", False) if info else False\n            ep_elapsed = time.time() - ep_t0\n            print(f\"  ep {ep_idx}: {ep_env_steps} env steps, {ep_decision_points} decision points, \"\n                  f\"success={success}, {ep_elapsed:.1f}s\")\n\n        env.close()\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n\n    obs_features_all = torch.stack(all_obs_features)\n    actions_K_all = torch.stack(all_actions_K)\n    expert_actions_all = torch.stack(all_expert_actions)\n\n    save_path = output_dir / \"precomputed.pt\"\n    torch.save({\n        \"obs_features\": obs_features_all,\n        \"actions_K\": actions_K_all,\n        \"expert_action\": expert_actions_all,\n        \"metadata\": {\n            \"suite\": args.suite,\n            \"K\": args.K,\n            \"n_episodes\": args.n_episodes,\n            \"total_decision_points\": total_decision_points,\n            \"total_env_steps\": total_env_steps,\n            \"chunk_size\": chunk_size,\n            \"action_dim\": int(precomputer.config.action_feature.shape[0]),\n            \"obs_dim\": precomputer.vlm_hidden_size,\n            \"seed\": args.seed,\n        }\n    }, save_path)\n\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(f\"Saved to {save_path}\")\n    print(f\"obs_features:  {obs_features_all.shape}\")\n    print(f\"actions_K:     {actions_K_all.shape}\")\n    print(f\"expert_action: {expert_actions_all.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Decision pts:  {total_decision_points}\")\n    print(f\"Env steps:     {total_env_steps}\")\n    print(f\"Elapsed:       {elapsed:.1f}s ({elapsed/max(total_decision_points,1):.2f}s/decision_point)\")\n\n    # --- Sanity checks ---\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    print(\"Sanity Checks\")\n    print(f\"  obs_features range: [{obs_features_all.min():.4f}, {obs_features_all.max():.4f}]\")\n    print(f\"  obs_features NaN: {obs_features_all.isnan().any().item()}\")\n    print(f\"  obs_features Inf: {obs_features_all.isinf().any().item()}\")\n    print(f\"  actions_K range:  [{actions_K_all.min():.4f}, {actions_K_all.max():.4f}]\")\n    print(f\"  actions_K NaN:    {actions_K_all.isnan().any().item()}\")\n\n    # K-sample diversity: pairwise L2 on first chunk step\n    a = actions_K_all[:, :, 0, :]  # (N, K, 7) — first step of each chunk\n    K = args.K\n    dists = []\n    for i in range(K):\n        for j in range(i + 1, K):\n            d = (a[:, i] - a[:, j]).norm(dim=-1)\n            dists.append(d.mean().item())\n    mean_pw_l2 = np.mean(dists) if dists else 0\n    print(f\"  Pairwise L2 (K={K}, step 0): {mean_pw_l2:.6f}\")\n\n    # Per-DOF std across K samples\n    per_dof_std = actions_K_all[:, :, 0, :].std(dim=1).mean(dim=0)\n    print(f\"  Per-DOF std (K samples, step 0): {per_dof_std.tolist()}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","numLines":393,"startLine":1,"totalLines":393}}}

Missing image 180° flip (primary — causes 0% SR)**  \nlerobot's `LiberoProcessorStep` applies `torch.flip(img, dims=[2, 3])` (flip both H and W) before feeding images to the model. The precompute script skips this, so the model gets upside-down/mirrored images and produces garbage actions.\n\n**2. Wrong success key (secondary — incorrect SR reporting)**  \nrobosuite's `_post_action()` returns an empty info dict. `info.get(\"success\", False)` always returns `False`. Should call `env.check_success()` instead.\n\nFixing now."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6565,"cache_read_input_tokens":83280,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":6565},"output_tokens":13,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2e5005b7-4383-4494-8f3b-f04ce9f94618","uuid":"cdc4f0dd-d04e-4b1a-9c95-bf8fc4f7db19"}
